# Cyclone UI (Light High Contrast) Components

Use documented components and examples instead of creating one-off replacements.

## @cyclone-ui/accordion

A collapsible Accordion compound component (Accordion.Item, Item.Header, Header.Heading, Item.Content) built on Tamagui's accordion. It supports single or multiple open items, default, separated, bordered and ghost variants, numbered headers, toggle or chevron icons with a configurable side, and an up or down direction. Use it for FAQs or grouped sections that users expand.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/accordion`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.3.11`

### Dependencies

- `@cyclone-ui/heading-text`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/icons`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/vectors`: `workspace:*`
- `@stryke/type-checks`: `catalog:`
- `@stryke/types`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/accordion`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/collapsible`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/core`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/group`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/helpers`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/stacks`: `catalog:`

### Development Dependencies

- `@cyclone-ui/body-text`: `workspace:*`
- `@storybook/react-vite`: `catalog:`
- `react`: `catalog:`
- `react-dom`: `catalog:`
- `react-native`: `catalog:`

## @cyclone-ui/alert

An inline alert banner with compound parts (Alert.Icon, Alert.Content.Heading, Alert.Content.Body, Alert.Close). Its type prop (base, brand, danger, warning, info, success, discovery, positive, negative) sets the color theme and default icon. Use it to show status or feedback messages in the page.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/alert`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.4.11`

### Dependencies

- `@cyclone-ui/body-text`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/button`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/container`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/heading-text`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/helpers`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/icons`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/themeable-icon`: `workspace:*`

- `@tamagui/group`: `catalog:`

## @cyclone-ui/alert-dialog

A modal alert dialog built on the Cyclone UI Dialog, with an overlay, a theme-based status icon, and compound Trigger, Container, Icon, Content (Heading, Body), Action and Close parts. Use it to confirm a destructive action or show an important success, warning or error message.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/alert-dialog`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.4.11`

### Dependencies

- `@cyclone-ui/dialog`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/icons`: `workspace:*`

## @cyclone-ui/anchor

A heading wrapper that renders its children next to a permalink icon. The icon shows on hover or focus and links to href on web, or opens the URL with Linking on native. Use it to make headings and sections deep-linkable.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/anchor`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.0.10`

### Dependencies

- `@cyclone-ui/icons`: `workspace:*`
- `@tamagui/core`: `catalog:`

## @cyclone-ui/badge

A Badge that wraps an element and pins a small count or dot to one of its corners. It follows MUI's API: anchorOrigin, max (shows 99+ by default), showZero, invisible, rectangular or circular overlap, standard or dot variants, and palette or theme colors. Use it for notification counts and status indicators.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/badge`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.3.11`

### Dependencies

- `@cyclone-ui/state`: `workspace:*`
- `@tamagui/core`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/web`: `catalog:`

## @cyclone-ui/body-text

A themed paragraph text component built on Tamagui's SizableText that renders a p element using the body typography tokens. It has md and sm sizes and an optional bold weight. Use it for standard body copy and as a base for other text components.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/body-text`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.5.11`

### Dependencies

- `@cyclone-ui/helpers`: `workspace:*`

## @cyclone-ui/breadcrumb

A breadcrumb trail of Breadcrumb.Item links followed by the current page name. It offers chevron, double-chevron or slash separators, inverse and subtle styles, and sizes. When the trail is longer than maxItems, the middle links collapse into a popover menu.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/breadcrumb`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.4.12`

### Dependencies

- `@cyclone-ui/button`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/helpers`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/icons`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/label-text`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/link`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/popover`: `workspace:*`
- `@tamagui/core`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/create-context`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/group`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/helpers`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/stacks`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/text`: `catalog:`

### Development Dependencies

- `react`: `catalog:`

## @cyclone-ui/button

A pressable button with Button.Text and Button.Icon subcomponents and primary, secondary, tertiary, outlined, ghost, link and cascade hover variants. It supports sizes, circular and rounded shapes, ringed and disabled states, press ripples, and rendering as an anchor with href.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/button`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.6.11`

## @cyclone-ui/bytes-text

A BodyText-based text component that renders a numeric byte count as a human-readable size (such as 1.5 kB) using pretty-bytes. Children that are not numbers are shown as 0. Use it to display file sizes, upload sizes or storage usage.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/bytes-text`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.5.11`

### Dependencies

- `@cyclone-ui/body-text`: `workspace:*`
- `@stryke/string-format`: `catalog:`
- `@tamagui/core`: `catalog:`

### Development Dependencies

- `@storybook/react-vite`: `catalog:`
- `react`: `catalog:`
- `react-dom`: `catalog:`
- `react-native`: `catalog:`

## @cyclone-ui/callout

A highlighted content panel with a theme-colored gradient and diagonal pattern background. Compound parts are Callout.Header (with Eyebrow, Heading and Icon) and Callout.Body, and the icon is picked from the theme by default. Use it to draw attention to important notes or featured content.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/callout`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.4.11`

## @cyclone-ui/card

A themed card container with a gradient background and an optional accent color. Compound parts include Header (Eyebrow, Heading, Icon), Body and Footer.Link, and the footer link's arrow animates when the card is hovered. Use it to present a summary or feature with a call-to-action link.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/card`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.6.11`

## @cyclone-ui/checkbox

A themed checkbox built on the Tamagui Checkbox, showing a check or a minus indicator for the indeterminate state. It supports form control sizes, circular, focused and disabled states, and validation colors when placed inside a Field.

- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.5.11`

## @cyclone-ui/checkbox-field

A form-bound CheckboxField compound component (Label, Link, Control, Details) that connects a Checkbox to the Cyclone UI form state. The form handles checked value, focus, blur, disabled and size, and the label shows a lock icon when the field is disabled. Use it for boolean inputs inside a Form.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/checkbox-field`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.1.11`

### Dependencies

- `@cyclone-ui/checkbox`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/field`: `workspace:*`
- `@cyclone-ui/state`: `workspace:*`

## @cyclone-ui/circular-progress

A circular progress indicator drawn with SVG on top of Tamagui's Progress primitive. It takes value and max props, a sm, md, lg or xl size (or any size token), and a theme color for the indicator arc. Use it to show determinate progress in a compact space.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/circular-progress`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.0.9`

## @cyclone-ui/collapsible

A single disclosure panel built on Accordion, with Collapsible.Header and Collapsible.Content subcomponents and a chevron icon. Open state can be controlled with a boolean value or left uncontrolled with defaultValue. Use it to show and hide one section of content.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/collapsible`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.3.7`

### Dependencies

- `@cyclone-ui/accordion`: `workspace:*`

## @cyclone-ui/file-tree

Folders animate open and closed, can start open with defaultOpen, accept custom icons and openIcon, and can be toggled with Enter or Space. It uses ARIA tree and treeitem roles. Use it to show directory or project structures.

## @cyclone-ui/otp-input-field

Focus moves with typing, Backspace and the arrow keys, pasted codes fill the inputs, and autocomplete uses one-time-code. onComplete fires when every digit is filled. ## @cyclone-ui/page

A Page layout compound component with Banner, TopNav (Start, Middle and End slots), SideNav, SideNavToggle, Main and Panel areas. The SideNav can be collapsed and resized; it shows as a hover flyout when collapsed on desktop and as an overlay on mobile. The Panel can also be resized. The page includes skip links and a Ctrl+[ shortcut. Use it as the shell of an application page.

## @cyclone-ui/tag-picker

A TagPicker input that turns typed text into removable tags. Enter adds a trimmed, unique tag, Backspace removes the last tag, and each tag has an accessible remove button. It can be controlled or uncontrolled (value, defaultValue, onChange) and supports size, variant, tagVariant and disabled props, plus validation styling. The package also exports tag list helpers. Use it for free-form keyword or label entry.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/tag-picker`
- **Type:** `component`

## @cyclone-ui/timeline

A Timeline that renders a list of events joined by a line, vertically or horizontally. Pass the events as value and render each with content, plus optional opposite (e.g. a date on the other side of the line) and marker render functions. align places the line left, right, top, bottom or alternating; TimelineMarker is the default dot for custom markers. Use it for order tracking, changelogs and activity feeds.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/timeline`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.0.1`

## @cyclone-ui/tag-picker

A TagPicker input that turns typed text into removable tags. Enter adds a trimmed, unique tag, Backspace removes the last tag, and each tag has an accessible remove button. It can be controlled or uncontrolled (value, defaultValue, onChange) and supports size, variant, tagVariant and disabled props, plus validation styling. The package also exports tag list helpers. Use it for free-form keyword or label entry.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/tag-picker`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.0.6`

It includes ThemedIcon and a getIconByTheme helper that pick a matching status icon (danger, warning, info, discovery, success, positive, negative) or a lock icon when disabled. ## @cyclone-ui/timeline

A Timeline that renders a list of events joined by a line, vertically or horizontally. Pass the events as value and render each with content, plus optional opposite (e.g. a date on the other side of the line) and marker render functions. align places the line left, right, top, bottom or alternating; TimelineMarker is the default dot for custom markers. Use it for order tracking, changelogs and activity feeds.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/timeline`
- **Type:** `component`

## @cyclone-ui/accordion

A collapsible Accordion compound component (Accordion.Item, Item.Header, Header.Heading, Item.Content) built on Tamagui's accordion. It supports single or multiple open items, default, separated, bordered and ghost variants, numbered headers, toggle or chevron icons with a configurable side, and an up or down direction. Use it for FAQs or grouped sections that users expand.

## @cyclone-ui/card

A themed card container with a gradient background and an optional accent color. Compound parts include Header (Eyebrow, Heading, Icon), Body and Footer.Link, and the footer link's arrow animates when the card is hovered. Use it to present a summary or feature with a call-to-action link.

## @cyclone-ui/checkbox-field

A form-bound CheckboxField compound component (Label, Link, Control, Details) that connects a Checkbox to the Cyclone UI form state. The form handles checked value, focus, blur, disabled and size, and the label shows a lock icon when the field is disabled. Use it for boolean inputs inside a Form.

## @cyclone-ui/confetti-burst

## @cyclone-ui/feedback

A Feedback control that pairs a like toggle with a dislike toggle. Pressing dislike opens a popover form asking for an optional comment. It can be controlled or uncontrolled (value, defaultValue, onValueChange), and it also has onCommentSubmit, a custom comment label and placeholder, disabled and size props. Use it to collect quick thumbs-up/down feedback on content.

## @cyclone-ui/tag-picker

A TagPicker input that turns typed text into removable tags. Enter adds a trimmed, unique tag, Backspace removes the last tag, and each tag has an accessible remove button. It can be controlled or uncontrolled (value, defaultValue, onChange) and supports size, variant, tagVariant and disabled props, plus validation styling. The package also exports tag list helpers. Use it for free-form keyword or label entry.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/tag-picker`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.0.6`

## @cyclone-ui/timeline

A Timeline that renders a list of events joined by a line, vertically or horizontally. Pass the events as value and render each with content, plus optional opposite (e.g. a date on the other side of the line) and marker render functions. align places the line left, right, top, bottom or alternating; TimelineMarker is the default dot for custom markers. Use it for order tracking, changelogs and activity feeds.

# Cyclone UI (Dark High Contrast) Components

Use documented components and examples instead of creating one-off replacements.

## @cyclone-ui/accordion

A collapsible Accordion compound component (Accordion.Item, Item.Header, Header.Heading, Item.Content) built on Tamagui's accordion. It supports single or multiple open items, default, separated, bordered and ghost variants, numbered headers, toggle or chevron icons with a configurable side, and an up or down direction. Use it for FAQs or grouped sections that users expand.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/accordion`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.3.11`

## @cyclone-ui/badge

## @cyclone-ui/breadcrumb

A breadcrumb trail of Breadcrumb.Item links followed by the current page name. It offers chevron, double-chevron or slash separators, inverse and subtle styles, and sizes. When the trail is longer than maxItems, the middle links collapse into a popover menu.

## @cyclone-ui/bytes-text

A BodyText-based text component that renders a numeric byte count as a human-readable size (such as 1.5 kB) using pretty-bytes. Children that are not numbers are shown as 0. Use it to display file sizes, upload sizes or storage usage.

## @cyclone-ui/page

A Page layout compound component with Banner, TopNav (Start, Middle and End slots), SideNav, SideNavToggle, Main and Panel areas. The SideNav can be collapsed and resized; it shows as a hover flyout when collapsed on desktop and as an overlay on mobile. The Panel can also be resized. ## @cyclone-ui/tag-picker

A TagPicker input that turns typed text into removable tags. Enter adds a trimmed, unique tag, Backspace removes the last tag, and each tag has an accessible remove button. It can be controlled or uncontrolled (value, defaultValue, onChange) and supports size, variant, tagVariant and disabled props, plus validation styling. The package also exports tag list helpers. Use it for free-form keyword or label entry.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/tag-picker`

## @cyclone-ui/timeline

A Timeline that renders a list of events joined by a line, vertically or horizontally. Pass the events as value and render each with content, plus optional opposite (e.g. a date on the other side of the line) and marker render functions. align places the line left, right, top, bottom or alternating; TimelineMarker is the default dot for custom markers. ## @cyclone-ui/badge

It follows MUI's API: anchorOrigin, max (shows 99+ by default), showZero, invisible, rectangular or circular overlap, standard or dot variants, and palette or theme colors. ## @cyclone-ui/feedback

A Feedback control that pairs a like toggle with a dislike toggle. Pressing dislike opens a popover form asking for an optional comment. It can be controlled or uncontrolled (value, defaultValue, onValueChange), and it also has onCommentSubmit, a custom comment label and placeholder, disabled and size props. Use it to collect quick thumbs-up/down feedback on content.

## @cyclone-ui/tag-picker

A TagPicker input that turns typed text into removable tags. Enter adds a trimmed, unique tag, Backspace removes the last tag, and each tag has an accessible remove button. It can be controlled or uncontrolled (value, defaultValue, onChange) and supports size, variant, tagVariant and disabled props, plus validation styling. The package also exports tag list helpers. Use it for free-form keyword or label entry.

## @cyclone-ui/timeline

A Timeline that renders a list of events joined by a line, vertically or horizontally. Pass the events as value and render each with content, plus optional opposite (e.g. a date on the other side of the line) and marker render functions. align places the line left, right, top, bottom or alternating; TimelineMarker is the default dot for custom markers. Use it for order tracking, changelogs and activity feeds.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/timeline`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.0.1`

## @cyclone-ui/accordion

It supports single or multiple open items, default, separated, bordered and ghost variants, numbered headers, toggle or chevron icons with a configurable side, and an up or down direction. ## @cyclone-ui/table-of-contents

A page navigation list built from an array of heading items, each with a title, a hash URL and a depth. On web it highlights the current section as the user scrolls and draws an indented rail showing the active heading path, and its motion respects reduced motion. Use it beside long documents.

## @cyclone-ui/tag-picker

A TagPicker input that turns typed text into removable tags. Enter adds a trimmed, unique tag, Backspace removes the last tag, and each tag has an accessible remove button. It can be controlled or uncontrolled (value, defaultValue, onChange) and supports size, variant, tagVariant and disabled props, plus validation styling. The package also exports tag list helpers. Use it for free-form keyword or label entry.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/tag-picker`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.0.6`

## @cyclone-ui/timeline

A Timeline that renders a list of events joined by a line, vertically or horizontally. Pass the events as value and render each with content, plus optional opposite (e.g. a date on the other side of the line) and marker render functions. align places the line left, right, top, bottom or alternating; TimelineMarker is the default dot for custom markers. Use it for order tracking, changelogs and activity feeds.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/timeline`
- **Type:** `component`

## @cyclone-ui/visually-hidden

A wrapper that hides its content visually but keeps it available to screen readers. The visible, preserveDimensions and animate variants can reveal the content or keep its size. Use it for accessible labels and text that only assistive technology should announce.

- **Name:** `@cyclone-ui/visually-hidden`
- **Type:** `component`
- **Version:** `0.5.11`

### Dependencies

- `@tamagui/core`: `catalog:`

### Development Dependencies

- `react`: `catalog:`
- `react-dom`: `catalog:`
- `react-native`: `catalog:`
